# Enshrouded item ids

Generated by `tools/extract_items.py`. 4011 identified items, 245 without a name (see `unknown.csv`).

Ids are written the way you read them (big endian); in save files they are stored little endian. ✔ = checked in game.

Source: `override` checked by hand, `legacy` older community list, `internal` internal name from game data, `twin` armor stats entry named after its pair.

- [animal](#animal) (24)
- [armor](#armor) (581)
- [arrow](#arrow) (44)
- [block](#block) (64)
- [consumable](#consumable) (46)
- [cosmetic](#cosmetic) (126)
- [decoration](#decoration) (888)
- [food](#food) (97)
- [material](#material) (197)
- [other](#other) (1244)
- [prop](#prop) (145)
- [ring](#ring) (27)
- [shield](#shield) (7)
